([fahnaladitia/dashcrew](https://github.com/fahnaladitia/dashcrew/tree/HEAD/test/golden/sample-migrate-p3/p3/.agents/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)
- **strategic-compact**: - Running long sessions that approach context limits (200K+ tokens) - Working on multi-phase tasks (research → plan → implement → test) - Switching between unrelated tasks within the same session - After completing a major milestone and starting new work - When responses slow down or become less coherent (context pressure) ([gugug168/claudecode-tutorial](https://github.com/gugug168/claudecode-tutorial/tree/HEAD/everything-claude-code-learning/02-Skills-%E6%BA%90%E6%96%87%E4%BB%B6%E6%B3%A8%E9%87%8A%E7%89%88/strategic-compact) · ★1)

- **strategic-compact**: Suggests optimal compaction points during long sessions. Monitors tool call count and recommends manual /compact at phase transitions rather than arbitrary auto-compaction. ([hun99999/dotfiles-for-antigravity](https://github.com/hun99999/dotfiles-for-antigravity/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)
- **strategic-compact**: A phase-aware context management strategy that suggests manual compaction at logical task boundaries. ([jxoesneon/Ciel](https://github.com/jxoesneon/Ciel/tree/HEAD/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)
- **strategic-compact**: Use em sessões longas/multi-fase pra decidir QUANDO sugerir um /compact — em fronteiras naturais de fase, antes que o contexto encha e degrade a qualidade. ([Matheuscara/claude-code](https://github.com/Matheuscara/claude-code/tree/HEAD/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)
- **strategic-compact**: Invoke when starting, resuming, updating, closing, or handing off a multi-workstream task; create or maintain a five-field Strategic Compact for continuity. ([mfraile/naos-governance](https://github.com/mfraile/naos-governance/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)
- **strategic-compact**: Suggest manual /compact at logical workflow boundaries instead of relying on arbitrary auto-compaction. Use when the session approaches context limits, after completing a major milestone, when switching between unrelated tasks, or when responses become less coherent due to context pressure. Pairs with the suggest-compact.sh hook for automated threshold detection. ([samwang0723/claudecode-setup](https://github.com/samwang0723/claudecode-setup/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)
- **strategic-compact**: Checkpoint long, multi-phase work before context pressure or session handoff when losing constraints, evidence, or completed-item coverage would risk incorrect continuation. ([strmt7/ai_skills_collection_benchmarked](https://github.com/strmt7/ai_skills_collection_benchmarked/tree/HEAD/included/improved/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)
- **strategic-compact**: You're a pragmatic executor focused on shipping results and measuring impact. You use AI to amplify your effect and automation to eliminate busywork. ([surfingalien/my-claude-agents](https://github.com/surfingalien/my-claude-agents/tree/HEAD/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)
- **strategic-compact**: 策略性 Context 壓縮工具 - 在邏輯邊界建議手動 /compact，避免 auto-compaction 在不恰當時機打斷工作流。Use for: (1) 探索階段完成、即將進入實作時, (2) 完成一個 Milestone 後要開始下一個 Phase, (3) 長時間 debug 後恢復正常開發, (4) 大量工具呼叫後 Context 開始擁擠時。Use when: 用戶詢問何時應該 /compact、擔心 Context 被截斷、完成重要里程碑後想要清空無關 Context 時。 ([tarrragon/claude](https://github.com/tarrragon/claude/tree/HEAD/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **strategic-compact**: 長いセッションで手動 /compact を論理的な区切りで打つ判断支援。自動フックは使わない。compact 前に作業状態・次アクション・未解決事項・参照ファイルを会話内に明示し、消える前に文脈を守る。永続化が要る場合のみ既存 handoff 運用に従う。 ([tatsunoritojo/claude-code-project-starter](https://github.com/tatsunoritojo/claude-code-project-starter/tree/HEAD/home-claude/skills/strategic-compact) · ★1)

- **strategic-compact**: Suggests manual context compaction at logical workflow boundaries to preserve useful task context instead of relying on arbitrary auto-compaction. ([Aaditya93/Estate-CRM](https://github.com/Aaditya93/Estate-CRM/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/productivity/strategic-compact))
